# The Art of War
Aliases: Art Of War, Art of War, The Art Of War
Categories: Year of the Pig, Legendary items, Items, The Uprising
Year of the Pig Exclusive The Art of War is only obtainable during [[Year of the Pig]].
[The Art of War]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✔ Yes
[Shop]
Merchants: Pig Shop ([[Piggy]])
Buy: 5,000,000 [[Coins]] 256x (4 stacks (256 items total)) [[Shiny Token]] 1x [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]]
[Materials]
Raw Materials (All materials in their most basic form needed to obtain the item.): 25,600x (400 stacks (25,600 items total)) [[Raw Porkchop]] 256x [[Shiny Orb]] 5,000,000 [[Coins]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: THE_ART_OF_WAR
Tooltip: Combinable in Anvil; Grants +5 Strength when applied to a weapon. Can only be applied to an item once.; In the midst of chaos, there is also opportunity.; - Sun Tzu; LEGENDARY
In the midst of chaos, there is also opportunity.
— Sun Tzu
The Art of War is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item that can be applied to [[Weapons]]/[[Axes]] to grant a [[Strength]] bonus.
## Obtaining
The Art of War can be purchased from [[Piggy]] During the [[Year of the Pig]] for 5,000,000 [[Coins]], 256 (4 stacks) [[Shiny Token]], and 1 [[Enchanted Cooked Porkchop]].
## Usage
The Art of War can be applied to [[Weapons]]/[[Axes]] via an anvil to grant +5 [[Strength]]. This can only be done once per item.
## Trivia
- The Art of War was originally obtainable from the [[Piggy|Technoshop]] whilst [[Resistance Leader|Technoblade]] was mayor.
  - It was also available to purchase from the [[Piggy|Pig Shop]] during the 3rd, 4th, and 5th [[SkyBlock Anniversary|SkyBlock Anniversaries]].
- The Art of War is a reference to Technoblade's [[SkyBlock]] series, The Great Potato War, where he often quoted and used Sun Tzu's philosophies.

# Bouncy Beach Ball
Categories: Year of the Seal, Epic items, Items
Year of the Seal Exclusive Bouncy Beach Ball is only obtainable during [[Year of the Seal]].
[Bouncy Beach Ball]
Type: Item
Rarity: EPIC ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Fishing]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BOUNCY_BEACH_BALL
Tooltip: Can be fished up during the Year of the Seal.; Ability: Bounce Bonanza RIGHT CLICK Toss this ball into the air and try bouncing it on your head to earn Fishy Treats. The more bounces, the bigger the rewards!; This item is consumed on use.; EPIC
A Bouncy Beach Ball is an [[Rarity|EPIC]] item obtained from fishing. It is used to start a minigame about bouncing the ball to obtain [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].
## Obtaining
A Bouncy Beach Ball can be obtained while [[Fishing]] anywhere during the [[Year of the Seal]]. It has a 25% (1 in 4) chance to replace a GOOD CATCH or GOOD JUNK CATCH.
|Treasure Chance|Catch Chance|
|-|-|
|5 [[Treasure Chance]]|1.11%; (1 in 89.9)|
|15 [[Treasure Chance]]|3.34%; (1 in 30.0)|
|30 [[Treasure Chance]]|6.68%; (1 in 15.0)|
When caught, the Bouncy Beach Ball grants +160 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]].
## Usage
A Bouncy Beach Ball can be used to start a minigame where the player must bounce the Bouncy Beach Ball off of their head continuously, where each bounce counts towards [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]]. Up to 20 [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]] can be obtained from a Bouncy Beach Ball after 40 bounces. Any bounces past 40 will provide no additional treats.
A Bouncy Beach Ball cannot be used on the [[Private Island]], [[The Garden]] or the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Calculation
The number of treats received is calculated depending on the number of bounces, rounded down:
|Bounces (n)|Fishy Treats|
|-|-|
|0|1|
|1–3|n|
|4–27|n+42|
|28–39|n+203|
|40+|20|
If the player has a [[Blended Fish Mixin]] active, the number of treats received is increased by 3.
### Calculator
calcpage  = Calculator:Fishy Treats
 template  = Template:Calculator/Fishy Treats
 form      = fishyTreatsCalcForm
 calcname  = Fishy Treats Calculator
 result    = inner

 param     = num|Number|0|number|0-
 param     = calc_type|Type|Bounces to treats|select|Bounces to treats,Treats to bounces
Loading Calculator...
If the calculator fails to load up, please report to an Administrator.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Associated Text|
|-|-|
|When initially using the item|BOUNCE BONANZA! Keep the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for as long as you can by bouncing it on your head!|
|After achieving 0–1 bounces in a row|AW MAN! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned 1 Fishy Treat!|
|After achieving 2–5 bounces in a row|DECENT! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned <#> Fishy Treats!|
|After achieving 6–15 bounces in a row|GOOD! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned <#> Fishy Treats!|
|After achieving 16–25 bounces in a row|AMAZING! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned <#> Fishy Treats!|
|After achieving 26–39 bounces in a row|IMPRESSIVE! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned <#> Fishy Treats!|
|After achieving 40 or more bounces in a row|INSANE! You kept the Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for <#> bounces and earned 20 Fishy Treats!|
|When the ball hits the wall|Oops! Your Bouncy Beach Ball hit the wall after <#> bounces, still earning you <#> Fishy Treats!|
|When the ball hits the ceiling|Oops! Your Bouncy Beach Ball hit the ceiling after <#> bounces, still earning you <#> Fishy Treats!|
|When attempting to use the item on a disallowed island|You can't use this here!|
|When attempting to use the item in a space that is too small|This item does not have enough space!|
## Trivia
- The maximum amount of bounces possible without bugs is 187.
  - This is because the velocity of a Bouncy Beach Ball becomes so great that it passes through the player's hitbox before detection.

[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Feb 13, 2025|Bouncy Beach Ball now pops upon touching a Cobweb ([[Web]]).|

The Frozen Amulet is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Necklaces|Necklace]].
## Obtaining
The Frozen Amulet can be purchased from [[Lukas the Aquarist]] for 256 (4 stacks) [[Fishy Treat]].

Necklace:

# Giant Bouncy Beach Ball
Categories: Year of the Seal, Legendary items, Items, Article stubs, More info needed
Year of the Seal Exclusive Giant Bouncy Beach Ball is only obtainable during [[Year of the Seal]].
[Giant Bouncy Beach Ball]
Type: Item
Rarity: LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])
Source: [[Fishing]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: GIANT_BOUNCY_BEACH_BALL
Tooltip: Can be fished up during the Year of the Seal.; Ability: Bounce Bonanza EX RIGHT CLICK Toss this ball and try bouncing it on your head as many times as you can in 60s to earn Fishy Treats. The more players that participate, the bigger the rewards!; This item is consumed on use.; LEGENDARY
A Giant Bouncy Beach Ball is a [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] item obtained from fishing. It is used to start a version of the [[Bouncy Beach Ball]] minigame where multiple players can bounce the ball to earn [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].
## Obtaining
A Giant Bouncy Beach Ball can be obtained while [[Fishing]] anywhere during the [[Year of the Seal]]. It has a 15% (1 in 6.66) chance to replace a GREAT CATCH or GREAT JUNK CATCH.
|Treasure Chance|Catch Chance|
|-|-|
|5 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.075%; (1 in 1,333.3)|
|15 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.225%; (1 in 444.4)|
|30 [[Treasure Chance]]|0.45%; (1 in 222.2)|
When caught, the Giant Bouncy Beach Ball grants +300 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]].
## Usage
A Giant Bouncy Beach Ball can be used to start a minigame where the player must bounce the Giant Bouncy Beach Ball off their head or other player's heads as many times as possible within 60 seconds.
A Giant Bouncy Beach Ball cannot be used on the [[Private Island]], [[The Garden]] or the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Calculation
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Needs information on other numbers of participating players (up to 60).
See more stubs.
The player who launched the Giant Bouncy Beach Ball receives a number of [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]] depending on the number of players who participated. Anyone else who participated receives 1 [[Fishy Treat]].
|Participants|Fishy Treats|
|-|-|
|0–1|5|
|2–4|9|
|5–7|12|
|8–14|15|
|15+|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|
## Game Messages
This section needs more information. Please help expand it. Note: Add text strings that occur at different numbers of bounces achieved, as well as their exact ranges.
See more stubs.
- Grammar and/or punctuation mistakes are intentional, as they appear so in the dialogue in-game.
|Condition|Associated Text|
|-|-|
|When initially using the item|BOUNCE BONANZA EX! Keep the Giant Bouncy Beach Ball in the air for 60 seconds by bouncing it on your and other players heads!|
|After achieving a low amount of bounces in a row|DECENT! The Giant Bouncy Beach Ball was kept in the air for <#> bounces and <#> unique players have participated, rewarding you with <#> extra Fishy Treats!|
|When attempting to use on disallowed island|You can't use this here!|
## Trivia
- The theoretical maximum amount of bounces for a Giant Bouncy Beach Ball is 1,200.
  - Minecraft runs 20 game ticks per second and the Giant Bouncy Beach Ball has a 60 second time limit.

Description: Sells [[Fishing]]-related items in exchange for Fishy Treats ([[Fishy Treat]]) during the [[Year of the Seal]].
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 16.5 71 -8.5
Lukas the Aquarist is an [[NPC]] located in the [[Village]] during the [[Year of the Seal]]. They sell fishing-themed items for [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].
## Dialogue
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|First Interaction|[NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Ahoy, friend! [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: The Year of the Seal has arrived at last - it's about time, right? [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Listen up, here's the deal. [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Fish up those fancy Bouncy Beach Balls from Treasure, and you'll get to play a minigame that's as fun as bouncing on your nose! [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Rack up some Fishy Treats, then swing by here and treat yourself to something shiny. [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Got it? [NPC] Lukas the Aquarist: Now, get out there and make a splash!|
## Trivia
- It costs 1,728 (27 stacks) [[Fishy Treat]] to purchase the [[Frozen Amulet]], [[Seal Talisman]] and [[Seal Ring]], [[Seal Pet]], and all [[Small Seal Plushie|Seal Plushies]].
  - This would require a minimum of 87 [[Bouncy Beach Ball]], assuming each [[Bouncy Beach Ball]] bounces 40 times.
  - To afford 4 Tidal I books, it will take 256 (4 stacks) [[Fishy Treat]], which will require a minimum of 13 [[Bouncy Beach Ball]].
## Gallery
- Lukas the Aquarist, as seen in the Hub

# Medium Seal Plushie
Categories: Year of the Seal, Rare items, Items
Year of the Seal Exclusive Medium Seal Plushie is only obtainable during [[Year of the Seal]].
[Medium Seal Plushie]
Type: Item
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Tiers]
← Previous: [[Small Seal Plushie]]
Next →: [[Large Seal Plushie]]
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✕ No
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: MEDIUM_SEAL_PLUSHIE
Tooltip: Use at Kat to upgrade Seal Pets from Uncommon to Rare.; RARE
A Medium Seal Plushie is a [[Rarity|RARE]] item that can be used to upgrade a [[Seal Pet]] to [[Rarity|RARE]] at [[Kat]].
## Obtaining
A Medium Seal Plushie can be purchased from [[Lukas the Aquarist]] for 128 (2 stacks) [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]] during the [[Year of the Seal]].
## Usage
A Medium Seal Plushie can be used to upgrade an [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] [[Seal Pet]] to [[Rarity|RARE]].

A Seal Pet is a [[Fishing]] [[Pets|Pet]].
## Obtaining
A Seal Pet can be purchased from [[Lukas the Aquarist]] during the [[Year of the Seal]] for 128 (2 stacks) [[Fishy Treat|Fishy Treats]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|100 [[Coins]]|1x [[Small Seal Plushie]]|1 Minute|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|1x [[Medium Seal Plushie]]|1 Hour|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|1x [[Large Seal Plushie]]|1 Day|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|1x [[Giant Seal Plushie]]|3 Days|

|-|-|-|-|
|[[Sea Leopard Seal Skin]]|[[Sea Leopard Seal Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|[[Fire Sale]] - June 13, 2026 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
|[[Pup Seal Skin]]|[[Pup Seal Skin]]|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Seasonal Bundles ([[Taylor]]) - Fall 2026 - 650 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|
## Tips
- When partaking in your first [[Year of the Seal]], it is recommended to only upgrade your Seal Pet up to [[Rarity|EPIC]], otherwise it will be unavailable for a large portion of the event.
  - Once you have purchased the other items ( [[Frozen Amulet]], [[Seal Talisman]] and [[Seal Ring]]), or toward the end of the event, you can start upgrading it to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]].

[NPC] Alcina: To get ingredients, you need to kill certain types of mobs found throughout SkyBlock. [NPC] Alcina: Each type of mob will drop its own ingredient. [NPC] Alcina: Bring ingredients to me and you can make special stews, which you can consume to gain permanent stat buffs and other rewards. # Blended Fish Mixin
Aliases: Seal Mixin
Categories: Year of the Witch, Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Year of the Seal
Year of the Witch Exclusive Blended Fish Mixin is only obtainable during [[Year of the Witch]].
[Blended Fish Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Agnes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: BLENDED_FISH_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Grants +25% more Fishy Treats from bouncing Bouncy Beach Ball and Giant Bouncy Beach Ball.; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
A Blended Fish Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
Up to 3 Blended Fish Mixin can be purchased from [[Agnes]] for 128 (2 stacks) [[Year of the Witch|Waterbourne Lice]] during the [[Year of the Witch]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Blended Fish Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player +3 more [[Fishy Treat]] from [[Bouncy Beach Ball|Bouncy Beach Balls]] and [[Giant Bouncy Beach Ball|Giant Bouncy Beach Balls]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Blended Fish Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a [[God Potion]] is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 18, 2026|0.24.2 RC|Added Blended Fish Mixin.|
|Feb 23, 2026|Changed Blended Fish Mixin effect from +25% to +3.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Added Blended Fish Mixin.|
Linked from: [[Bouncy Beach Ball]], [[Mixins]], [[Year of the Witch]]

# Pig Brain Mixin
Aliases: Pig Mixin
Categories: Year of the Witch, Rare items, Items, Mixins, Brewing ingredients, Year of the Pig
Year of the Witch Exclusive Pig Brain Mixin is only obtainable during [[Year of the Witch]].
[Pig Brain Mixin]
Type: Mixin
Rarity: RARE ([[Rarity]])
[Item Properties]
Salable (Whether or not the item(s) can be sold to an NPC. If an item is not salable but has enchantments on it, it becomes salable.): ✕ No
Tradeable: ✔ Yes
Auctionable: ✔ Yes
Museum (Whether or not the item(s) can be donated to the Museum): ✕ No
[Shop]
Merchants: [[Agnes]]
[Item Metadata]
Item ID: PIG_BRAIN_MIXIN
Tooltip: Brewing Ingredient; Mixins provide a buff that can be added to God Potions in a brewing stand and lasts for the full duration.; Gain a 10% chance to receive an extra item from Shiny Pigs!; Duration: 36h 0m; The duration of Mixins can be stacked!; Right-click to consume!; (Requires active Booster Cookie); RARE
A Pig Brain Mixin is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Mixins|Mixin]] that can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant it an additional effect for its duration.
## Obtaining
Up to 3 Pig Brain Mixin can be purchased from [[Agnes]] for 128 (2 stacks) [[Year of the Witch|Frog Legs]] during the [[Year of the Witch]].
## Usage
### Brewing
A Pig Brain Mixin can be brewed with a [[God Potion]] to grant the player a +10% chance to drop a 2nd item from [[Shiny Pig|Shiny Pigs]]. This effect lasts for the [[God Potion]]'s entire duration.
|Ingredients|Brewing ([[Potions]]) Layout|
|-|-|
|[[God Potion]]|Recipe: Pig Brain Mixin → God Potion, God Potion, God Potion|
### Consuming
If the [[Booster Cookie|Cookie Buff]] is active, Mixins can also be consumed directly by right clicking them. This will provide 3x their normal duration (since a Mixin can be applied to up to 3 [[God Potion|God Potions]]). The timer is separate from the [[God Potion]] timer and is paused if a [[God Potion]] is no longer active. The player cannot consume Mixins they do not meet the requirements for.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Feb 18, 2026|0.24.2 RC|Added Pig Brain Mixin.|
|Feb 23, 2026|Removed +25% more [[Coins]] from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]) effect.|
|Feb 23, 2026|Added +50% chance to drop a [[Potato Talisman]] or [[Blood God Crest]] from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]) effect.|
|Sep 30, 2026|(Experimental) Added Pities ([[Pity and RNG Meters]]) to the [[Blood God Crest]] (1,000 Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])) and to the [[Potato Talisman]] (2,000 Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]])), both affected by the effect of the [[Pig Brain Mixin]] Pig Brain Mixin.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Added Pig Brain Mixin.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Removed +50% chance to drop a [[Potato Talisman]] or [[Blood God Crest]] from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]) effect.|
|Sep 1, 2026|0.27.1|Added +10% chance to drop a 2nd item from Shiny Pigs ([[Shiny Pig]]) effect.|

Each player may only purchase a maximum of one Witch Pet per profile in each [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]].

## Kat Caretaking Costs
[[Kat]] can upgrade all rarities of this [[Pets|pet]].
- Costs listed are at [Lv1]. For each level the pet has, there is a 0.3% discount.
- The time listed is in real life days, not in-game time. It can be reduced by up to 10% using the [[Attributes|Kat's Favorite]] attribute.
|Rarity Upgrade|Cost [[Coins]]|Cost Materials|Time|
|-|-|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]]) → UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|100 [[Coins]]|1x [[Small Witch Cauldron]]|1 Minute|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]]) → RARE ([[Rarity]])|10k [[Coins]]|1x [[Medium Witch Cauldron]]|1 Hour|
|RARE ([[Rarity]]) → EPIC ([[Rarity]])|250k [[Coins]]|1x [[Large Witch Cauldron]]|1 Day|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]]) → LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|500k [[Coins]]|1x [[Giant Witch Cauldron]]|3 Days|
## Trivia
- The Toil and Trouble perk references an excerpt from MacBeth: "Double, double toil and trouble; Fire burn and cauldron bubble".
- Witch Pet is the only pet that grants a Wisdom stat as a pet stat, rather than an ability.

# Accessory Power/Mechanics
Aliases: Magical Power/Mechanics
Each [[Accessories|Accessory]] the player has in their [[Accessory Bag]] gives an amount of [[Accessory Power]] based on its [[Rarity]]. The amount of [[Accessory Power]] the player has determines the player's Accessory Power multiplier, which is calculated with this formula:
  Stats Multiplier=29.97×(ln⁡(0.0019×(Accessory Power)+1))1.2
The stats given by the player's Accessory Power are multiplied by this multiplier. Dungeon Accessories give double [[Accessory Power]] inside [[Dungeons]].
|Rarity|[[Accessory Power]]|
|-|-|
|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|3|
|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|5|
|RARE ([[Rarity]])|8|
|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|12|
|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|16|
|MYTHIC ([[Rarity]])|22|
|DIVINE ([[Rarity]])|28|
|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|3|
|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|5|
|ULTIMATE ([[Rarity]])|1|
|ADMIN ([[Rarity]])|22|
There are no [[Rarity|DIVINE]] or [[Rarity|ULTIMATE]] accessories obtainable without glitches (besides the [[Celestial Starstone]] which is a [[Rift Dimension|Rift]]-exclusive accessory and does not grant [[Accessory Power]]), but they are listed for completeness.
For each [[Accessory Power]] the player has, they earn [[SkyBlock Levels|1 SkyBlock XP]].

For every 9 [[All-in Aloe Fragment|All-in Aloe Fragments]], the mutation grants 1 [[All-in Aloe]] instead.
Higher stages offer higher raw drops, but lower drops per unit of time due to cumulative reset risk and longer growth duration. On average, it is best to harvest at Growth Stage 14 to maximize drops. However, for completion, Growth Stage 11 is better as it will grant 2 [[All-in Aloe]], enough for analysis and purchasing [[Rose Dragon Pet|Rose Dragon Egg]] from [[Ludleth]]. Realistically, if the intent is purely growing as many aloes as possible to maximize profit then it is recommended to harvest at some point past Growth Stage 14 due to the possibility of missing cycles (via being asleep or busy), as well as reducing cost of replanting mutations used to grow the aloe.

# Alpha Hypixel Network
Aliases: AHN, Ahn, Alpha, Alpha Network, HAN, Han, Hypixel Alpha Network
- 14/250 The Alpha Hypixel Network is currently open with a limit of 250 players.; When the data was last updated, there were 14 players online, so there is currently no queue.
SkyBlock Time [[Time Systems|🛈]]
Alpha Network
:
of , Year
Image for the Alpha Hypixel Network used in the introduction announcement.
The server as seen in the Minecraft Multiplayer tab.
The Alpha Hypixel Network (acronymized as AHN), or Alpha Network, is a testing server that allows players to test upcoming updates for games on the Hypixel Network. This server is on alpha.hypixel.net.
The Alpha Hypixel Network hosts testing sessions in which the server is open players to test specified subjects, usually upcoming updates or features for specific games. Currently, [[SkyBlock]] is the only supported game on the server, and upcoming updates for the game are planned to be tested in the server. A rank requirement may be present for joining the Alpha Network.
## Mechanic
The Server Noticeboard on the Alpha Network Game Lobby
A Server Noticeboard was added to the Alpha Network on 22 November 2022, with information about the session objective.
The Alpha Network Game Lobby was added on 6 December 2022, replacing the Hypixel Game Lobby, a map from the main server.
### Alpha Network Reset
When an Alpha Network Reset (or Network Reset) happens:
- Any previous progress on the Alpha Network is wiped.
- Upon joining SkyBlock, a player will enter the profile they last played on the main server.
Upon joining the first profile after a reset, a Transfer of Progress may happen:
- All progress made by the player on all profiles on the main server will be transferred the Alpha Network, including all built structures on the private island of each profile.
- Player begins receiving game messages about [[Achievements]]/[[Collections]] unlocks, and [[Skills]]/[[Pets|pet]] leveling up, until they are the same as on its corresponding profile on the main server.
- Note: The above is the intended behavior. It can happen that a player joins the Alpha Network without a proper transfer of progress. That is, they start as if they play SkyBlock for the first time.
Upon joining the first profile after a reset, this message appears:
Welcome to Hypixel SkyBlock on the Alpha Network! ALPHA! Please report bugs at https://hypixel.net/bugs
### Time on Alpha Network
Before 21 August 2022, the [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Time]] on Alpha Network was the same as that of the main server.
On 21 August 2022, the SkyBlock Time on Alpha Network was shifted by exactly 211,680 seconds / 58 hours and 48 minutes in Earth's duration or 176.4 days / 5 months, 21 days, 9 hours and 36 minutes in SkyBlock's duration ahead of the main server. This change was persistent to future sessions.
On 9 April 2024, the Alpha server had a different time offset from what it previously had, presumably because the content being tested was only available during the first quarter of each SkyBlock year (spring). It was modified to be exactly 210 SkyBlock days (70 hours) ahead of the regular server, and the server was opened to the public around the start of Early Spring 2, Year 343.
The time offset was later changed on an unknown date (June 10, 2024 at the latest) to match the main server exactly.
### General Points to Note
- Progress made after the Transfer of Progress is branched out from the main server, and later progress on either alpha or main server will not affect the other.
- New features or new testing sessions on the Alpha Network do not necessarily come with a Network Reset; It is on admin's decision to trigger a Network Reset.
- The player limit of during each session, shown on server listings, can change on admin's decision. This limits the number of players that can play the Alpha Network at the same time.
- The [[Auction House]] generally does not work on the alpha server
## Observed Behavior
The observed similarities and differences of the Alpha Network with the main server is listed below.
### Events
All [[Events]] seem to also happen on the Alpha Hypixel Network. For example:
- Every [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] are scheduled at the same time with the same crops as the main server.
- During [[Traveling Zoo]], [[Oringo]] has the same [[Traveling Zoo/Events|pet stocks]] as the main server.
- [[Bingo]] events run at the same time and with the same tasks as the main server.
- [[Mayor Election|Mayor Elections]] run separately from those on the main server, but special mayors appear in the same years as on the main server. See [[Mayor Election|Mayor Election#On the Alpha Hypixel Network]].
### Others
- [[Centauri]] is an [[NPC]] that grants [[Coins]], [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]], item upgrades and update-featured items. Can also be accessed with the /centauri [[Commands|command]].
- The [[Chocolate Factory]] has a button that gives 1,000,000,000 [[Chocolate]] for testing.
- [[Salesman]] does not appear in the [[Hub Island|Hub]].
- [[Simon]] appeared in the [[Hub Island|Hub]] in Year 200 handing out [[Century Cakes]].
- [[Hub Selector]] lists only 12 servers, instead of 28 as in the main server.
- [[Dungeon Hub Selector]] lists only 3 servers, instead of 28 as in the main server.
- In a given year where [[Candidate Jerry]] is mayor, the [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse|Perkpocalypse]] Mayor Rotation Cycle is the same as in the main server.
- Players cannot bid on [[Shen's Auction]] or [[Regalia Room]] auctions.
Changes on the main server may not be reflected on the Alpha Network immediately:
- When [[Simon]] was removed on the main server on 11 April 2022, he was not removed at the same time on the Alpha Network. When the [[Security Sloth]] was added to the main server on 24 May 2022, it was not added immediately on the Alpha Network. Instead, both changes were done when a session opened on 9 June 2022.
- When the main server [[Hub Island|Hub]] was Easter-decorated in 2022, the Alpha Network Hub was not changed.

## Trivia
- The server was announced on May 5th, 2020, along with the return of linking to the Hypixel Discord through the /discord [[Commands|Command]], and access to the #admin-lounge channel for testers.
- When attempting to use /skyblock after a Network Reset, if the player was on a [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] [[Profile]] when joining, this message can appear: You were kicked while joining that server! Cannot join that island on a Stranded profile! Clicking on the SkyBlock Lobby NPC does not trigger this warning and player is able to enter SkyBlock normally.
- When the player is awarded Bingo Points from previous [[Bingo]] events, this message can appear: You have been given [#] Bingo Points that were missing from the [n-th] Bingo.
- Currently and before 26 July 2022, when attempting to join the server when it is closed or when rank is insufficient, the connection screen will show: Failed to connect to the server The Hypixel Alpha server is currently closed!
- In the time between 26 July 2022 and Early 2023, When rank is insufficient (current), the player will still be able to join the Lobby in Alpha Network, but this message shows when attempting to enter SkyBlock: You were kicked while joining that server! Can't find your SkyBlock info! Try /play skyblock!
- When a player is idle for too long, the server will attempt to send them to the Prototype Lobby, which has a capacity of 0 player on the Alpha Network. The following messages will appear: You are being transferred to the Prototype Lobby for being AFK! That lobby is currently full! Try again later.
- Trying to join without having played skyblock will result in the following message: Failed cloning your SkyBlock data: Your SkyBlock data was not migrated yet. Please join SkyBlock on mc.hypixel.net first!
- Buying a rank while connected to the Alpha server will not provide the rank until the main server is joined again.

# Animal Axes
Aliases: Animal Axe, Mooshroom Axe, Mooshroom Cow Axe
[Animal Axes]
Animal Axes are a category of swords that increase specific drops from [[Animal|Animals]].
## List
|Icon|Name|Rarity|Stats|Drops|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Basic Chicken Axe]]|[[Basic Chicken Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Chicken]], [[Feather]]|
|[[Advanced Chicken Axe]]|[[Advanced Chicken Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|(same as above)|
|[[Basic Cow Axe]]|[[Basic Cow Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Beef]]|
|[[Advanced Cow Axe]]|[[Advanced Cow Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Beef]]|
|[[Basic Mooshroom Axe]]|[[Basic Mooshroom Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]), [[Leather]]|
|[[Advanced Mooshroom Axe]]|[[Advanced Mooshroom Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|(same as above)|
|[[Basic Pig Axe]]|[[Basic Pig Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Porkchop]]|
|[[Advanced Pig Axe]]|[[Advanced Pig Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Porkchop]]|
|[[Basic Rabbit Axe]]|[[Basic Rabbit Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Rabbit]], [[Rabbit Hide]], [[Rabbit's Foot]]|
|[[Advanced Rabbit Axe]]|[[Advanced Rabbit Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|(same as above)|
|[[Basic Sheep Axe]]|[[Basic Sheep Axe]]|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|10 [[Damage]]|[[Raw Mutton]], White Wool ([[Wool]])|
|[[Advanced Sheep Axe]]|[[Advanced Sheep Axe]]|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|20 [[Damage]]|(same as above)|
## Obtaining
Basic Animal Axes can be purchased from the [[Husbandry Merchant]] for 100 coins.
Advanced Animal Axes can be purchased from [[Jake]] for 1,000 [[Coins]] and the corresponding Basic Animal Axe after bringing him the corresponding animal.
## Usage
Basic Animal Axes add +1 to the drops and Advanced Animal Axes add +2 to the drops.
They can be upgraded with all [[Swords|Sword]] upgrades.
## Trivia
- Animal Axes used to use the [[Axes|Axe]] item type before being converted into [[Swords]].

# Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse
Aliases: Candidate Jerry/Mayor Rotation, Perkpocalypse
Perkpocalypse: Activates all perks of another mayor every 18 SkyBlock days (6 hours)
When [[Candidate Jerry]] is elected, his Perkpocalypse ability will give a set of perks from another mayor, and it rotates every 6 Earth hours (18 SkyBlock days). The timetable below will be updated each time Candidate Jerry is elected, and recorded for future reference.
## Latest Timetable
The following shows the latest timetable. This section is updated each time when Candidate Jerry is elected, or is going to be elected. View historical data instead
e [[Candidate Jerry/Perkpocalypse/Year 521|v]]
The election end time of Late Spring 27th, Year 521 on the Main Server is Tue Oct 20 2026 15:15:00 GMT+0000.
If Candidate Jerry is elected on the Main Server, the Perkpocalypse event listing would be as follows:
Year 521 [edit] [refresh]:
|Perks From|Appearances (Time in UTC)|
|-|-|
|[[Cole]]|20 Oct — 15:15➤21:15 22 Oct — 03:15➤09:15 23 Oct — 15:15➤21:15 25 Oct — 03:15➤09:15|
|[[Aatrox]]|20 Oct — 21:15➤03:15† 22 Oct — 09:15➤15:15 23 Oct — 21:15➤03:15† 25 Oct — 09:15➤15:15|
|[[Paul]]|21 Oct — 03:15➤09:15 22 Oct — 15:15➤21:15 24 Oct — 03:15➤09:15 25 Oct — 15:15➤19:15|
|[[Diana]]|21 Oct — 09:15➤15:15 22 Oct — 21:15➤03:15† 24 Oct — 09:15➤15:15|
|[[Finnegan]]|21 Oct — 15:15➤21:15 23 Oct — 03:15➤09:15 24 Oct — 15:15➤21:15|
|[[Marina]]|21 Oct — 21:15➤03:15† 23 Oct — 09:15➤15:15 24 Oct — 21:15➤03:15†|
[†: Time on the next day.]
Note: [[Finnegan]]'s Grand Feast perk is not activated by Jerry.

# Capsule
[Capsule]
Capsules are [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] items that can only be interacted with in the [[Critter Safari]].
## Capsules
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Source|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Critter Capsule]]|[[Critter Capsule]]|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|From the [[Safari Manager]] upon entering the [[Critter Safari]]; quantity varies with tier of Safari Ticket entered|
|[[Masterful Critter Capsule]]|[[Masterful Critter Capsule]]|SPECIAL ([[Rarity]])|1 [[Masterful Critter Capsule]] is given upon entering the [[Critter Safari]] if the player has Critter Master ([[Archie]]) purchased from [[Archie]]|
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jul 10, 2026|Added Capsules.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 9, 2026|Teased a 'new way of hunting', which was later revealed to be through Capsules.|
|Aug 4, 2026|0.27|Added Capsules.|

Size: 1x1
Secrets: 1
Crypts: 0
The Basement is a room that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Contents

  - 1 [[Wither Essence]]
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:

# Catacombs Rooms/Cathedral
Aliases: Cathedral Room
[Cathedral]
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Size: 2x2
Structure: Square
Secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]]): 8
Crypts ([[Crypt Undead]]): 5
Cathedral is a [[Catacombs Rooms|Room]] that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Structure
- Cathedral is square-shaped, containing 3 levels held together by a crossing on Level 0.
- It can be split into 3 components :
  - 1 crossing at its center, featuring 4 gates numbered from Ⅰ through Ⅳ.
  - 4 aisles prolonging the crossing in cardinal directions with 2 reaching the arcs on Level 0 and Level 1, each.
  - 4 arcs encircling the crossing with 2 on Level 0 and Level 1, each.
### Crossing
- Its gates are numbered – in a clockwise manner – Ⅱ, Ⅳ, Ⅰ, Ⅲ.
### Aisles
- Aisles Ⅰ and Ⅱ lead to arcs Ⅰ and Ⅱ on Level 0, respectively.
- Aisles Ⅲ and Ⅳ consist of stairwells, each eventually branching out into 2 flights of stairs, reaching Level 1:
  - Aisle Ⅲ leads to arc Ⅲ;
  - Aisle Ⅳ leads to arc Ⅳ on its left and arc Ⅲ on its right.
### Arcs
- Arcs Ⅰ and Ⅱ lead to Level -1.
- Arcs Ⅱ and Ⅳ communicate vertically.
- Arc Ⅳ is the smallest.
## Mechanisms
- Tripwire Traps are found on Levels 0 and 1:
  - On Level 0, it is specific of arc Ⅱ;
  - On Level 1, it is specific of arc Ⅲ.
- An Arrow Trap is found on the right branch of aisle Ⅳ.
## Mobs
- A [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] spawns at the crossing on Level 0.
## Contents
- 8 [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]]:
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level -1 in a hidden room
    - accessed from arc Ⅰ, behind a [[Cracked Stone Bricks|Breakable Wall]] on Level -1.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Item]] on Level -1 under a flight of stairs nearby a [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water Pond]]
    - accessed from arc Ⅱ, beneath a [[Planks|Wooden Bridge]].
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level -1 in a hidden room
    - accessed from arc Ⅱ, beneath the leftmost [[Crypt Undead|Crypt]].
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret Bat]] on Level 0 in a wall niche,
    - accessed from arc Ⅱ, around the inner corner of a [[Oak Log|Wooden Pillar]].
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Item]] on the upper layer of Level 1 in a wall niche,
    - accessed from arc Ⅲ, more specifically the catwalk above the Tripwire Trap.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Item]] on the upper layer of Level 1 atop an [[Andesite]] ledge,
    - found on arc Ⅲ, near a network of [[Web|Cobwebs]] across the ceiling.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level 1 in a hidden room
    - accessed from arc Ⅳ, behind a [[Cracked Stone Bricks|Breakable Wall]].
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret Bat]] on Level 1 in the outermost corner
    - accessed from arc Ⅳ, across the pit linking arcs Ⅱ and Ⅳ.
- 5 [[Crypt Undead|Crypts]] (5 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], 0 [[Prince]]) :
  - 1 [[Crypt Undead]] on arc Ⅰ
  - 3 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]] on arc Ⅱ, including 1 on Level -1 near a nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret Chest]]
  - 1 [[Crypt Undead]] on arc Ⅲ.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Released The Catacombs Dungeon.|
Linked from: [[Catacombs Rooms]], [[Catacombs Rooms/Catacombs Rooms Table]], [[Dungeon Secrets]]

# Catacombs Rooms/Diagonal
Aliases: Diagonal Room
[Diagonal]
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Size: 1x3
Secrets: 4
Crypts: 3
Diagonal is a room that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Structure
- Diagonal pans across 2 floors overlapping each other in an helix shape, linked by staircases.
- 2 rooms lie underground.
## Contents
- 4 [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]]:
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level -1 in a small room riddled with traps,
    - accessed from Level 0 using [[Vines]] hidden below the nolink=y Staircase.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level -1 in a room filled with [[Bookshelf|Bookshelves]],
    - accessed from Level 0 using [[Ladder|Ladders]] hidden in a corner of the “main” room filled with [[Bookshelf|Bookshelves]].
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chest]] on Level 1 in a rooom containing [[Block of Gold|Blockss of Gold]],
    - accessed from Level 1 behind [[Leaves|Oak Leaves]] partially covering a nearby nolink=y [[Crypts|Crypt]] at one end of the room.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret Bat]] on Level 0 in a small room containing [[Grass]] and a nolink=y Water Pond,
    - accessed from Level 0 behind a nolink=y Crate in a nolink=y wooden-floored room.
- 3 [[Crypt Undead|Crypts]] (3 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], 0 [[Prince]]) on Level 1 at the opposite ends of the room.
- 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] on Level 0 in the same nolink=y Water Pond.
- 1 [[Journal]] on Level 1 behind the iron bars of a cell.
## Renders
- These renders focus on relative positions between the points of interest.
- On Level 0, a thin strip of blocks has been willingly left out as a marker in respect to the [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret]] below.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Released The Catacombs Dungeon.|
Linked from: [[Catacombs Rooms]], [[Catacombs Rooms/Catacombs Rooms Table]], [[Dungeon Secrets]]

# Catacombs Rooms/Supertall
Aliases: Supertall Room
[Supertall]
Location: The Catacombs ([[Catacombs]])
Size: 2x2
Structure: Square
Secrets ([[Dungeon Secrets]]): 6
Crypts ([[Crypt Undead]]): 6
Supertall is a [[Catacombs Rooms|Room]] that can be found in [[Catacombs|The Catacombs]].
## Structure
- Supertall stretches over 4 levels, linked by shafts. It is shaped like a tube :
  - 4 levels : make up its "walls", ring-shaped, stacked on each other.
  - 4 shafts :
    - 3 occupy its center, connecting levels 1-2, 2-3 and 3-4.
    - 1 shaft connects levels 1-4, within the "walls".
- Supertall anatomy is notoriously complex among [[Catacombs Rooms]], making navigation challenging (see Skinned Renders below).
## Mechanisms
All levels contain a [[Lever]]. All shafts are accessed from a Gate.
- Flicking a [[Lever]] unlocks the Gate on its respective level.
- [[Dungeonbreaker]] renders these obsolete, excepted on Level 3 as its [[Lever]] is required to unlock a nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Secret Chest]] on Level 4.
Tripwire Traps and Fire Spitters Traps can be found.
## Mobs
- A [[Minibosses|MINIBOSS]] spawns on Level 1.
- [[Sniper|Snipers]] spawn in the shaft connecting Levels 3 and 4.
## Contents
- 6 [[Dungeon Secrets|Secrets]]:
  - 1 [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bat]] on Level 1 in a hidden room,
    - accessed from a tight [[Leaves|Oak Leaves]] passage on Level 1.
  - 1 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Item]] on Level 2 in a secluded room,
    - accessed from a spiraling [[Dirt]] path on Level 2,
    - or from collapsing the [[Andesite]] floor in front of a wall of Fire Spitters Traps on Level 3.
  - 1 [[Bat (disambiguation)|Bat]] on Level 3 in the dead-end
    - easily accessed from the [[Dirt]] path to the left of the uphill one on Level 3.
  - 3 nolink=y [[Dungeon Secrets|Chests]] on Level 4 ,
    - accessed from the shaft linking Levels 3 and 4.
    - unlocked from flicking a [[Lever]] in a hidden room on the higher end of Level 3 (see Level 3 minimaps below).
- 6 [[Crypt Undead|Crypts]] (4 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]], 2 [[Prince|Princes]]) :
  - 2 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]] on Level 1
  - 1 [[Prince]] on Level 2, behind a [[Cracked Stone Bricks]] wall
  - 2 [[Crypt Undead|Crypt Undeads]] on Level 3
  - 1 [[Prince]] on Level 4.
- 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] on Level 3 .
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jul 7, 2020|0.8|Released The Catacombs Dungeon.|
Linked from: [[Catacombs Rooms]], [[Catacombs Rooms/Catacombs Rooms Table]], [[Dungeon Secrets]]

# Century Cakes/Countdown
The message box and countdown timer automatically update for each century celebration. They are shown here and on the following pages when it is within two SkyBlock years of the start of a year divisible by 100.
- [[Simon]]
- Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki
- [[Century Cakes]]
- [[Hypixel SkyBlock Wiki/sandbox]]
- Template:MainPage/SideWidgets
- [[Incredible Raffle Box]]
- [[Raffle of the Century]]

# Changelog/2020/April 2
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
## New Feature
- Added [[Catacombs Entrance]]

# Changelog/2020/August 1
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- [[Mort]] won't give [[Dungeons|Dungeon]] Orbs on [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] and up if the player does not already have one.

# Changelog/2020/December 2
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- Added [[Chill the Fish]] as a catchable drop in the Main Lobby fishing (for a limited time)

# Changelog/2020/December 4
- First Changelog
- Second Changelog

# Changelog/2020/July 30
- First Changelog
- Second Changelog

# Changelog/2020/March 27
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- Added a 20% [[Skills|Skill]] Experience and 10 [[Magic Find]] Bonus as a weekend bonus. This was extended for an unknown limited duration.

# Changelog/2021/January 11
- Please note that this is not an official patch note!
[[Candidate Jerry]] has been elected, and with him, new items have been released.
- [[Jerry Stone]], a [[Reforge Stones|Reforge Stone]] exclusive for the [[Aspect of the Jerry]].
- [[Jerry-chine Gun]], a new weapon that shoots jerry heads.
- [[Jerry 3D Glasses]], a [[Pet Items|Pet Item]] to upgrade the [[Jerry Pet]]'s rarity.
- [[Jerry Candy]], a new consumable.
- [[Jerry Box|Jerry Boxes]], a new loot box, similar to [[Gifts]]
- [[Runes|Jerry Rune III]], a new rune.
- A new [[Accessories|Accessory]] line that decreases [[Intelligence]].
  - [[Green Jerry Talisman]]
  - [[Blue Jerry Talisman]]
  - [[Purple Jerry Talisman]]
  - [[Golden Jerry Artifact]]
Added [[Fuming Potato Book]] and [[Recombobulator 3000]] to [[The Catacombs - Floor VII]] loot chests.

# Changelog/2021/January 13
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- Patched a glitch that allowed players to easily kill [[Spooky Festival]] mobs with [[Thunderlord]] and [[Thunderbolt]].

# Changelog/2021/January 14
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
Added [[Jerry Candy]]'s effects to the [[God Potion]]:
- +100 [[Health]]
- +20 [[Strength]]
- +2 [[Ferocity]]
- +100 [[Intelligence]]
- +3 [[Magic Find]]
Additional BugFixes
- Fixed issue that didn't allow players to combine Turbo Cocoa books.
- Issue where [[Redstone Dust|Redstone]] showing as overmaxed out on [[Private Island|player's islands]] has been fixed
- Issue where [[Catacombs Mobs|Blood Room]] mobs were not spawning has been fixed
- Issue where the [[Experimentation Table]] was inverted has been been fixed
- Issue where the timer above [[Elizabeth]] in the [[Community Center]] wouldn't load has been fixed

# Changelog/2021/January 18
On January 18th 2021, the sorrow [[Drop Chance]] was decreased to 0.3 percent due to insanely efficient techniques appearing in order to kill [[Ghosts]].

# Changelog/2021/January 25
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- Fixed bug where you could use [[Aspect of the End]] or any blade with [[Necron's Blade Scrolls|Shadow Warp]] on it to teleport through iron bars.

# Changelog/2021/January 27
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- The [[Titanium Pickaxe]] now grants +310 [[Mining Speed]], a buff of 10 Mining Speed
- Your first [[Heart of the Mountain]] reset is free instead of costing 100,000 [[Coins]]
- The [[Mining Fortune]] perk now gives +5 mining fortune per level instead of +2
- The bug regarding not being able to claim some rewards for your [[Commissions|Commission]] Milestones has now been fixed, make sure to reclaim!

# Changelog/2021/January 6
~Please note this is not an official patch note!
- [[Mort]] won't give [[Dungeons|Dungeon Orbs]] anymore in [[The Catacombs - Floor III|Floor III]] and up if the player does not have one already.
- Reset [[Skills|Social XP]] Leaderboard
- Fixed Double Death Inventory Wipe
- Properly Resets [[Enchanting|Enchanting XP]] Cap
- Fixed Flying in [[The Catacombs - Floor VII|Floor VII]] ([[Tarantula Armor|Tarantula Boots]])
